Given - Book when rented has fixed charge for two days and extra charge for each day after that&#44; Latika paid 22 for 6 days while Anand paid 16 for 4 days Solution - Consider the fixed charge as x and extra charge as y&#44; then Charge for Latika - ₹ 22 = x + (days–2) y ₹ 22 = x + (6 – 2) y = x + 4y ........eq(i) Charge for Anand – ₹16 = x + (total days – 2) y = x + (4 – 2) y ₹16= x + 2 y .............eq (ii) Subtracting eq (ii) from eq (i) &#44; x + 4y – (x + 2y) = 22 – 16 .......[In ₹] x+4y-x-2y = 6 .........[In ₹] 4y – 2y = 6 2y = 6 y = 6/2 y = ₹3 Substituting value of y in eq (ii) ₹16 = x + 2 (₹3) ₹16 = x + ₹6 ₹16 - ₹6 = x ₹10 = x .. The fixed charge for first two days is ₹ 10 and additional charges for next days is ₹3 per day. | 28. Given - tan A + sin A = m; tan A – sin A = n To prove – m² – n² = 4 √mn Solution – first solving LHS&#44; LHS = (tan A + sin A)² – (tan A – sin A)² = tan² A + sin²A + 2tanAsinA – (tan²A + sin²A – 2tanAsinA) = tan²A + sin²A + 2tan Asin A – tan²A – sin² A + 2tan A sin A = 4 tanAsinA New solving RHS&#44; we get&#44; = 4√mn = 4√(tan A + sin A)(tan A-sin A) = 4√tan² A-sin² A = 4√((sin² A/cos² A)-sin² A) .......(tan²A-sin²Acos²A) = 4√((sin² A-cos² Asin² A)/cos² A) = 4√(sin² A(1-cos² A)/cos² A) = 4√(sin² A×sin² A/cos² A) .......[sin²A = 1 − cos²A] = 4 sin A× sin A / cos A = 4 tanAsinA ∴ LHS = RHS&#44; Hence proved | 32. Base proportionality theorem states that&#44; if a line is parallel to a side of a triangle which intersects the other sides into two distinct points&#44; then the line divides those sides in proportion for that triangle. Let ΔABC&#44; where line l is parallel to BC&#44; intersect AB at and AC at E To prove : AD/DB = AE/EC Proof: - Join BE&#44; CD and Draw EF ⊥ AB&#44; DG ⊥ CA Since EF is the height of AADE and ADBE Now&#44; Ar (AADE) = ½ (Base × Height) ½ × AD × EF Ar (ADBE) = ½ (Base × Height) ½ × DB × EF ar(AADE)/ar(ADBE) = (1/2×AD×EF)/(1/2×DBxEF) = AD/DB .....(i) Also&#44; ar(AADE)/ar(ADCE) = (1/2×AE×DG)/(1/2×EC×DG) = AE/EC .....(ii) But ADBE and ADCE are on the some base DE and between the same parallel ∴ ar(ADBE) = ar(ADCE) ......(iii) From (i)&#44; (ii) and (iii) we have AD/DB = AE/EC Hence proved <diagram_2> | 27. Base Proportionality theorem states that if a line is parallel to a side of a triangle which intersects the other sides into two diffrent points then the line divides those sides in proportion for that triangle Let ∆ABC where line l is parallel to BC&#44; intersect AB at D and AC at E To prove: AD/DB = AE/EC Proof :- Join BE&#44;CD and Draw EF ⊥ AB&#44; DG ⊥ CA Since EF is the height of ∆ADE and ∆DBE Now&#44; Ar(∆ADE) = 1/2 (Base x Height) = 1/2 x AD x EF Ar(∆DBE) = 1/2 (Base x Height) = 1/2 x DB x EF ∴ ar(∆ADE) / ar(∆DBE) = 1/2 x AD x EF / 1/2 x DB x EF = AD/DB --- (i) Also&#44; ar(∆ADE) = 1/2 x AE x DG --- (ii) ar(∆DCE) = 1/2 x EC x DG But ∆DBE and ∆DCE are on the same base DE and between the same parallel ∴ ar(∆DBE) = ar(∆DCE) ---(iii) from (i)&#44; (ii) and (iii) we have AD/DB = AE/EC Hence Proved | 1.0963 | 27.
